A02 FOUNDATIONAL TEST: REPORT (TEST-ONLY review evidence)

What this is: the isolated A02 engineering experiment Christian authorized, "I authorize the next bounded, isolated A02 engineering experiment." (received 10 November 2026): Steps 1 and 2 of the review pathway, run by the handoff and consumed by the checked configuration chain through a TEST-ONLY option no route passes; High Press Escape's approved identity and scoring rows restated by us as CANDIDATE; the reasoning-disconnection safeguards; the zero-, one- and two-manipulation runs; the earliest consequential blocker; a brief A02/TA02 comparison.
Standing: TEST-ONLY. Isolated: nothing a coach or an administrator can reach runs it. Not deployed. Not merged. Not an authorization, not approved wording, not a selection input. Engineering evidence only: no run here establishes ecological suitability or representative sufficiency. "This authorization is for the isolated development pathway. It does not authorize production changes or broader Stage 2 implementation." (received 10 November 2026)
Dates: Christian's reply about A02 is cited with 10 November 2026, the date it was pasted and filed in the sources repository; the date it was sent is not recorded there.
Quotation marks: double, Christian's words, verbatim, with the reply's date; single, ours (values, labels, engine sentences).
How each item is told, as Christian asked, "Please distinguish implemented and tested behavior from proposals, assumptions, and ecological suitability." (received 10 November 2026): IMPLEMENTED AND TESTED (what the code does, measured by a registered test of this build); OPEN (unresolved, or detected as an open disconnection: never a pass); PROPOSALS (ours, for Christian's decision); ASSUMPTIONS (ours, each labelled where it enters); ECOLOGICAL SUITABILITY (not established by any run).

FIRST, FOR CHRISTIAN'S DECISION: THE CONSTRAINT SELECTOR
- To bypass the numerical quota in the isolated experiment, the constraint selector's source file (generateSelection.ts) gained one TEST-ONLY parameter, a switch that is off unless a caller passes it, in 9 places (each held to the file's text by this report's test):
   generateSelection.ts:700-702: the size rule's function takes the authorized set's own size, null unless the switch is on
   generateSelection.ts:715-718: given that size, the size rule admits 1 up to it instead of 2 to 4 (the first of the two places the size rule lives)
   generateSelection.ts:784-785: the enumeration takes the list of sizes it tries
   generateSelection.ts:798: the enumeration reads that list
   generateSelection.ts:835: the enumeration tries the listed sizes instead of the fixed 2, 3 and 4 (the second place)
   generateSelection.ts:885-895: the selector takes the switch, off unless a caller passes it
   generateSelection.ts:1031-1037: the switch is honoured only for an evaluated authorization record and throws for any other pool; under it the sizes tried are 2, 3, 4 and the set's own size
   generateSelection.ts:1061: the sizes are handed to the enumeration
   generateSelection.ts:1083: the own size is handed to the size rule
- No route, controller or service passes it (a registered static test reads every non-test source file); with it off, the selector is unchanged in behaviour (the behaviour snapshot against main, recorded apart in the regression record).
- Question: is a TEST-ONLY parameter that no route passes within "I am not authorizing a production change to the constraint selector at this stage." (received 10 November 2026)? It is removable: deleting the 9 places returns the file to main's text, and the one- and two-manipulation runs of item 4 would then be refused by the size rule, as before.

KEY (the identifiers the frame and the seven items use; names read from the knowledge files)
- A01: the Learning Goal 'Play Out from the Back' (the planning model)
- A02: the Learning Goal 'Play Through Pressure' (the planning model)
- TA02: the Learning Goal 'Secure Possession' (the planning model)
- GP-015: the Game Problem 'Maintain Possession' (the Game Problem library)
- GP-002: the Game Problem 'Exploit Space' (the Game Problem library)
- GP-001: the Game Problem 'Create Space' (the Game Problem library)
- GF2: the Game Form 'Directional Possession Games' (the sport module, a DRAFT module whose Game Problem cells we restate as CANDIDATE)
- GF3: the Game Form 'Positional Play Games' (the sport module, a DRAFT module whose Game Problem cells we restate as CANDIDATE)
- GF7: the Game Form 'Channel Games' (the sport module, a DRAFT module whose Game Problem cells we restate as CANDIDATE)
- RPC-002: the Representative Performance Context 'High Press Escape' (the context library)
- RPC-003: the Representative Performance Context 'Attack Development' (the context library)
- CAR: the CAR Matrix: each Game Problem's rating of four affordance requirements, FOI 'Functional Object Interaction', OP 'Open Pathway', SA 'Support Availability', CIO 'Competitive Interaction Opportunity' (E: Essential)
- RPC-PROP-133: the approved primary scoring condition of High Press Escape (a row of the context library)
- RPC-PROP-134: the approved primary scoring condition of Attack Development (a row of the context library)
- RPC-PROP-018: a begin condition of High Press Escape (a row of the context library)
- RPC-REL-...: a relationship row of the context library (RPC-REL-075..078: the four approved primary scoring events of High Press Escape)
- RPC-ID-...: an identity row of the context library
- RPC-MAP-...: a mapping row of the context library staging a context's affordance target (each DEFERRED)
- RPC-002-...: an item of our CANDIDATE restatement of High Press Escape (TEST-ONLY draft store), each beside the approved row it restates in appendix A
- RPC-003-...: an item of our DRAFT (CANDIDATE) restatement of Attack Development (TEST-ONLY draft store, 27 October)
- c:restated:...: a configuration line of the built game: c:<contract>:<item>::<register row>, the item that forms it and the property it sets (V5 a scoring condition's referent, V6 its value)
- A02-0: the A02 run of the knowledge as it stands, with our CANDIDATE restatement (no TEST-ONLY treatment beyond the consumption and the draft store)
- A02-1: the A02 run with the origin and progression items withheld (TEST-ONLY)
- A02-1b: A02-1 with the control referent declared to need no further value (TEST-ONLY, not ruled) and the Laws held to our DRAFT classification
- A02-2: A02-1b with the TEST-ONLY stand-in that reads the offside law procedural
- A02-D: the A02 diagnostic: every condition item withheld (TEST-ONLY), the offside stand-in: plain GF2
- TA02-0: the TA02 run of the knowledge as it stands, with our DRAFT restatement of Attack Development (the reuse comparison)
- TA02-D: the TA02 diagnostic, as A02-D (the reuse comparison)
- L1: the legacy route: A02 on the production records, nothing consumed (the route the production chain takes)
- S4-...: a run of item 4: manipulations entered only through the DRAFT record's authorized set (NP Neutral Player, WZA Wide Zone Advantage, TR Turnover Reward, CDC Central Density Condition, IR Interception Reward; /no-bypass: without the size-rule bypass)
- OD-...: an open disconnection the safeguards detect, listed with its measured evidence in appendix B; printed as open, never as a pass
- TT-...: a TEST-ONLY treatment, labelled where it enters (appendix A and appendix D)
- NO-RESTATEMENT: the A02 run consumed with the DRAFT record only, no restatement of High Press Escape in the store (item 5)
- V5: the register row of a scoring condition's referent
- V6: the register row of a scoring condition's value
- LAW-11: Law 11 (Offside)
- LAW-8: Law 8 (The Start and Restart of Play)
- D3: Christian's decision D3 (received 4 November 2026): Game Form relationships are compatibility information unless approved knowledge establishes a preference
- M6: one of the eight A01 safeguards Christian approved: no further value required, explicitly unrestricted, and a necessary value missing stay three distinct states
- RC1.1: the version of the Representative Performance Context Library the program holds (its rows' last verified version)
- generateSelection.ts...: the constraint selector's source file, with line numbers; any other name ending .ts is an engine source file, with line numbers, and a short hexadecimal string after one is the commit that introduced the rule
- TEST-ONLY: code, data or a treatment no route, controller or service reaches
- RESTATEMENT-ONLY: a refusal whose cited items are all ours and unreviewed (Christian's second reply of 7 November, section 2)
- APPROVED_TRANSLATION: Step 1's basis: the goal's approved translation row in the planning model
- SELECTION_TRACE_RANKING: where the chain's record of the forms its scorer ranked is read (its selection trace)
- AUTHORIZATION_RECORD: the constraints' authority: the authorization record's set (here a DRAFT record of ours, no ruling field filled)
- ENGINE_LENS: an affordance lens the engine scores from the goal's wording: an engine input, not knowledge
- NO_COMPATIBLE_COMBINATION: the selector's refusal: no combination of lenses and constraints passed its checks
- FOUNDATION_CLAUSE: the selection check that a package holds a foundation constraint (with its keyword threshold)
- PACKAGE_ROW_O: the selection check that a package shows a clear relationship to the selected affordance (row o)
- CONSUMED: a verdict: the value equals the upstream one and the chain's own record shows it was handed that value
- AGREES_INDEPENDENTLY: a verdict: equal values with no record of consumption (a latent disconnection, never counted as consumption)
- CONTRADICTED: a verdict: the value used downstream differs from the upstream one
- REFUSED_ON_OWN_CHOICE: a verdict: the chain refused on its own choice before consuming the upstream one
- CARRIED: a verdict: an item constituting the clause reached derivation and its lines resolved in the built game
- CITED_NOT_CARRIED: a verdict: an approved row is cited (or displayed) while nothing carries it
- WITHHELD_BY_TREATMENT: a verdict: a declared TEST-ONLY treatment withheld it (a detected disconnection, never a pass)
- NOT_REACHED: a verdict: the run stopped before the decision
- NOT_CLASSIFIED: the requirement account's state for a scoring condition with no requirement bindings: never a pass
- NOT_AUTHORED: a restatement declaration: nothing authored the value
- UNDECLARED: a restatement declaration: whether a value is owed is not stated
- NO_FURTHER_VALUE: a declaration that a value needs no further referent: here only a TEST-ONLY treatment, not ruled
- OUTSIDE_BOUNDARY: an item recorded outside what the engine can check: it establishes nothing
- TEST ASSUMPTION: Christian's class 4: a stated test assumption, never knowledge
The appendices are the detailed texts the A02 tests generate; they use these identifiers and the chain's own line, item and row identifiers, read as the key's c:restated: entry says.

1. CONSUMPTION OF STEP 1 AND STEP 2
Christian asked: "Whether the downstream pathway now consumes the authorized Game Problem and Game Form results without independent wording-based substitution." (received 10 November 2026)
Answer (OURS): on the isolated pathway, yes, for the Game Problem and the Game Form: the chain builds on Step 2's form and carries Step 1's Game Problem, and its own record of what it was handed shows it on every run. Two readings still come from the wording or the routing beside them (open, below). The production route is unchanged and still picks A02's form from the wording.
Implemented and tested:
- A TEST-ONLY option of the checked chain hands it Step 1's resolved Game Problem and Step 2's SELECTED form: its candidate forms become the routed context's declared forms filtered to Step 2's form (never replaced by it), a form outside them is refused, and its constraints come only from the authorization record.
- The 5 A02 runs (A02-0, A02-1, A02-1b, A02-2, A02-D): Step 1 RESOLVED GP-015 at RPC-002 (APPROVED_TRANSLATION); Step 2 SELECTED GF2 (compatible: GF2); the chain built on GF2, its scorer handed only GF2; the handoff placed nothing.
- On 40 of the 41 experiment runs (every one that reaches the chain), the Game Problem, the Game Form and the constraints read CONSUMED (40, 40 and 40 runs): each value equals Step 1's or Step 2's and the chain's own record shows it was handed that value. The one other run (Step 1's primary swapped to GP-001) is reconsidered by Step 2, which retains GF2, GF3, GF7 with no basis to select, and stops before the chain.
- Dependency: with Step 1's primary swapped to GP-002 in a test copy of the knowledge (TEST ASSUMPTION), Step 2 selects GF7 and the chain builds on GF7 carrying GP-002; with GF2's GP-015 cell moved to GF7 there, Step 2 selects GF7 and the chain builds on GF7. The legacy route on the same two inputs stays on GF3 in both, its pick from the wording.
- Wording: with the goal's wording rewritten twice (in the knowledge copy and in the row the chain reads; restored, the restore asserted), Steps 1 and 2 and the consumed form do not move (identical), while the chain's own route moves from GF3 to GF2, which proves the rewrite reached the chain.
Open (detected and listed as open disconnections, never passes):
- OD-HINT-GAME-PROBLEM: the Game Problem reading that governs the engine lens hints and matched signals is still the planning model's (guided-selection.ts:228), not Step 1's: equal by agreement on the A02 runs, different where Step 1 differs
- OD-CONTEXT: the context is read by the chain from the planning routing itself; no record of consuming Step 1's context exists (equal by agreement: both read the same routing row)
- OD-A02-LEGACY-FORM: the known A02 failure: the chain selects its own GF3 from the wording against Step 2's SELECTED form; closed on the experiment path by the consumption, open on the production route (unchanged)
- the engine lenses still follow the wording, and on one two-manipulation set the wording decides the selection (item 3).
Proposals (OURS, for Christian's decision): whether the Game Problem reading that governs the engine's lens hints should also be taken from Step 1; it lives in the guided selection module, which this experiment leaves untouched by design.
Assumptions (OURS): the consumption is itself a TEST-ONLY treatment (TT-CONSUMED-STEPS); GF2's Game Problem cell keeps its grade, CANDIDATE.
Ecological suitability: not established; consuming GF2 shows structural compatibility only.

2. HIGH PRESS ESCAPE'S IDENTITY AND SCORING
Christian asked: "Whether the existing High Press Escape identity and scoring relationships can be carried into configuration, and what remains unsupported." (received 10 November 2026)
Answer (OURS): partly. The approved scoring events are carried into the built game; the clauses of the approved scoring condition enter derivation through our CANDIDATE restatement, but as the knowledge stands the build stops on two of them, and nothing in a built game carries established pressure.
Implemented and tested:
- Our CANDIDATE restatement of RPC-002 in the TEST-ONLY draft store: 11 items, each quoting its approved row verbatim (the store's gate refuses a copy that does not); none of it is in production knowledge. Rows restated: RPC-REL-075, RPC-REL-076, RPC-REL-077, RPC-REL-078, RPC-PROP-133, RPC-ID-006, RPC-PROP-018, RPC-ID-008.
- Carried: the four approved events, narrowed with GF2's own to ['line_crossed', 'target_zone_entered'] on the built event line (the chain records our item as narrowing it); the values 'beyond' and 'retains possession through the next action' enter derivation.
- A02-0, the knowledge as it stands: refused at structure on c:restated:RPC-002:RPC-002-04.a::V5 (the control clause's referent) and c:restated:RPC-002:RPC-002-02.a::V6 (the origin clause's value): RESTATEMENT-ONLY: a conservative refusal by our CANDIDATE interpretation, not a finding that the configuration violates approved knowledge (Christian's second reply of 7 November, section 2).
- The origin clause over the 41 experiment runs: NOT_REACHED on 29, WITHHELD_BY_TREATMENT on 12.
- The progression clause over the 41 experiment runs: NOT_REACHED on 29, WITHHELD_BY_TREATMENT on 12.
- The control clause over the 41 experiment runs: NOT_REACHED on 34, CARRIED on 4, WITHHELD_BY_TREATMENT on 3 (carried only under the TEST-ONLY declaration that its referent needs no further value, which Christian has not ruled).
- A02-D, the diagnostic (every condition item withheld): the chain would serve plain GF2 (8 sentences): structural compatibility only, not representative sufficiency.
Unsupported (UNRESOLVED, kept explicit, never closed by an assumption; where each stands: appendix A):
- the origin clause of the approved condition (RPC-PROP-133): the sequence begins under established coordinated pressure
- the referent of the progression clause: the pressing line
- the referent of the control clause: retains possession through the next action
- the scope of the condition
- coach wording for the condition
- the restart and change-of-possession half of the starting state
- the Laws for A02, offside (LAW-11)
- the identity rows RPC-ID-006, RPC-ID-007, RPC-ID-008 and RPC-ID-010
- established opposition pressure
- RPC-002's affordance targets (RPC-MAP-097..102)
Smallest specific source material (questions only, in order of consequence):
1. Christian's observable start, for an activity, of the origin clause: the sequence begins under established coordinated pressure [the next consequential gap]
2. whether the pressing line may be realized as a designated pressure reference, and how it is placed

Proposals: none; the questions above ask for Christian's source material and propose no knowledge.

3. THE SAFEGUARDS AGAINST REASONING DISCONNECTIONS
Christian asked: "Whether the proposed reasoning-disconnection safeguards detect the known A02 failure and comparable unauthorized substitutions." (received 10 November 2026)
Answer (OURS): yes, for the known failure and for each substitution tested; they also detect disconnections that remain open, listed below and never counted as passes.
Implemented and tested (one pure check over each run's result, run by registered tests; no library, layer or monitoring system):
- Decision ownership: an ownership table names the owner, input, output and authority of each of 9 decisions (appendix B).
- The known failure: on L1 the chain refuses at selection on its own GF3 before consuming Step 2's GF2: REFUSED_ON_OWN_CHOICE; the assertion every experiment run must pass fails on it: 'ownership: L1: Game Form eligibility and choice owned by Step 2, under D3 over CANDIDATE cells: REFUSED_ON_OWN_CHOICE: the chain refused at selection on its own choice GF3 before consuming Step 2's GF2'.
- A comparable substitution: a test-only wrapper that drops the handed decisions, on a wording where the chain's own pick equals GF2: the form reads AGREES_INDEPENDENTLY and the assertion fails on it; the same run with the decisions handed reads CONSUMED and passes.
- Dependency verification: a changed Game Problem and a moved cell are reconsidered by Step 2 and consumed (item 1); the planning model's hint-governing reading stays GP-015 when Step 1 says GP-002 (CONTRADICTED, listed).
- Safeguard B fails on OD-WORDING-LENSES: the engine lenses (ENGINE_LENS) follow the goal's wording on the consumed path; no owned decision moved with them on these runs; before: Possession Stability Opportunity, Space Creation Opportunity, Regain Opportunity; after: Line-Breaking Opportunity, Possession Stability Opportunity, Space Creation Opportunity.
- Safeguard B fails on OD-WORDING-FLIP: wording alone decides the selection of a non-empty authorized set on the consumed form: Neutral Player with Turnover Reward is selected under the goal's wording and refused under a reworded one; before: the goal's wording: selected on GF2 with Neutral Player and Turnover Reward, then refused at knowledge (the authorized constraint Turnover Reward has no contribution contract); after: the reversed and the neutral wording: refused at selection, NO_COMPATIBLE_COMBINATION on GF2 (row k on every lens combination); lenses none.
- Consumption tracing: with one cited event removed from our restatement, A02-2 moves from communication to realization, on the event line that item shaped; with A02-D's treatment declaration removed, its three clauses read CITED_NOT_CARRIED and the assertion fails on it; with GP-015's CAR ratings changed, the chain's result is identical (consulted, not determining).
- Over the 65 runs: 168 violation verdicts and 43 detected disconnections, none of them a pass.
Open disconnections (10; each with its evidence and runs in appendix B):
- OD-HINT-GAME-PROBLEM [experiment path]: the Game Problem reading that governs the engine lens hints and matched signals is still the planning model's (guided-selection.ts:228), not Step 1's: equal by agreement on the A02 runs, different where Step 1 differs
- OD-CONTEXT [experiment path]: the context is read by the chain from the planning routing itself; no record of consuming Step 1's context exists (equal by agreement: both read the same routing row)
- OD-DISPLAY [experiment path]: the account displays the approved scoring condition as in this activity (inThisActivity true) whenever the context's contract is among those the build reads, whether or not any clause is carried
- OD-PACKAGE-LENSES [experiment path]: after the form is consumed, a kept package check that reads the wording-derived lenses decides the selection (the foundation clause's keyword threshold on the primary lens; row o)
- OD-WORDING-FLIP [experiment path]: wording alone decides the selection of a non-empty authorized set on the consumed form: Neutral Player with Turnover Reward is selected under the goal's wording and refused under a reworded one
- OD-WORDING-LENSES [experiment path]: the engine lenses (ENGINE_LENS) follow the goal's wording on the consumed path; no owned decision moved with them on these runs
- OD-LEGACY-AGREEMENT [legacy route, reported only]: the legacy route consumes no upstream decision: wherever its own Game Problem, context, form or constraints equal the upstream ones, they agree independently
- OD-LEGACY-VOCABULARY [legacy route, reported only]: constraints built from a vocabulary pool where no record authorizes any
- OD-LEGACY-GAME-PROBLEM [legacy route, reported only]: the legacy route reads its own Game Problem, which differs from Step 1's
- OD-A02-LEGACY-FORM [legacy route, reported only]: the known A02 failure: the chain selects its own GF3 from the wording against Step 2's SELECTED form; closed on the experiment path by the consumption, open on the production route (unchanged)
Proposals (OURS, for Christian's decision): whether the kept package checks that read the wording-derived lenses fall under "Please preserve all independently justified structural, compatibility, and representative-integrity checks." (received 10 November 2026), or are a selection from wording the consumption is meant to remove; they are kept, enforced and reported here.
Assumptions (OURS): the verdicts, the ownership table and the attribution of each refusal to a check.
Ecological suitability: not addressed by the safeguards.

4. ZERO, ONE OR MORE MANIPULATIONS WITHOUT THE QUOTA
Christian asked: "Whether zero or one justified manipulation can be represented without the inherited numerical quota interfering." (received 10 November 2026)
Answer (OURS): zero and one manipulation can be represented without the quota interfering; no manipulation is justified for A02 in anything we hold, so what is shown is the representation, not a justified configuration.
Implemented and tested:
- Zero: the 5 A02 runs commit the DRAFT record's empty set (constraints CONSUMED, none built): representable, not shown to be justified; the record's finding is ours: no manipulation has an authorized relationship to RPC-002 and GP-015 in held knowledge.
- One: a lone Neutral Player in the DRAFT record's set (a TEST-ONLY relationship stand-in, TEST ASSUMPTION) is selected with the TEST-ONLY size-rule bypass (S4-NP: next stop structure) and refused without it (S4-NP/no-bypass: refused at selection, attributed to the size rule): the quota interfered, and the bypass removed only it.
- One, refused by kept checks: a lone Wide Zone Advantage or Turnover Reward is refused by the foundation clause; Central Density Condition, alone and with Interception Reward, by the package relationship check; each attributed by re-running the selector's own exported package check on GF2 and each lens combination (S4-WZA FOUNDATION_CLAUSE, S4-TR FOUNDATION_CLAUSE, S4-CDC PACKAGE_ROW_O, S4-CDC+IR PACKAGE_ROW_O).
- Two: Neutral Player with Wide Zone Advantage is selected whole (next stop structure); with Turnover Reward it is selected, then refused at knowledge (Turnover Reward has no contribution contract).
- Every line a manipulation brings rests on its stand-in, class 4 (TEST ASSUMPTION) at best: 7 lines for Neutral Player alone, 25 with Wide Zone Advantage (on the diagnostic base, where a game is built).
- Of the 14 selection checks only the numerical size rule is bypassed; every other check runs and enforces wherever its path is reached. On the empty-set runs the role mix, the package checks and the size rule are not run at all: the zero branch commits the empty set first, and its authority is Christian's general principle of 15 October 2026 (a constraint is not mandatory unless justified).
- Reported with source and authority, as Christian asked: the role mix: source generateSelection.ts:590-612, applied :839, 3980188, 3 May 2026; authority none found; engine rule; kept and enforced, not treated as canonical.
- Reported with source and authority, as Christian asked: the foundation clause (with its keyword threshold): source build-constraint-package.ts:614-621 (threshold :147-153); validate-constraint-package.ts:325-327, e60a740, 18 April 2026; authority none found; engine rule (our own comment calls it not ruled); kept and enforced, not treated as canonical.
Open: the wording decides one two-manipulation selection (item 3); the role mix cannot be attributed without an observer inside the selector, and none was added.
Proposals (OURS, for Christian's decision): whether the TEST-ONLY switch in the constraint selector may stay (the disclosure above); it is removable.
Assumptions (OURS): every manipulation here is a TEST-ONLY stand-in, never knowledge.
Ecological suitability: not established: selected means only that the kept checks did not refuse it.

Nothing was drawn from outside the goal's candidates. [Incorrect reasoning chronology or decision authority] (passed by NO-RESTATEMENT)

- Downstream, recorded and not investigated:
   A02-1 (origin and progression withheld, TEST-ONLY): structure [Missing knowledge]
   A02-1b (+ the control referent declared NO_FURTHER_VALUE, TEST-ONLY): validation [An unapproved or inaccurate interpretation; and Missing knowledge]

"An unapproved or inaccurate interpretation." "A broken relationship between existing knowledge components." "Incorrect reasoning chronology or decision authority." "A configuration or rendering implementation limitation."
Proposals: none; the smallest source material that would resolve it is question 1 of item 2, a request, not a proposal of knowledge.
Assumptions (OURS): the assignment of each stop to Christian's categories, and the order of the questions.
Ecological suitability: not addressed: the blocker is a gap of knowledge and of a carrier, met before any activity is built.

6. REUSE BETWEEN A02 AND TA02 (brief)
Christian asked: "Whether the existing knowledge relationships appear capable of supporting reasoning reuse between A02 and TA02, without requiring a new goal-family structure." (received 10 November 2026)
Answer (OURS): Measured, the Game Problem, affordance and Game Form reasoning is shared without any new structure: both goals resolve to GP-015 through their own approved translation rows, Step 1 consults the same CAR profile, Step 2 selects GF2 from the same relationship (its GP-015 cell graded CANDIDATE in both), and the same TEST-ONLY consumption carries both results to the chain. What would set the two contexts apart lives in each context's own rows (its identity, its opposition and its scoring condition), which the chain applies after the shared reasoning, through each context's restatement: the existing architecture already has that place. It does not execute yet: as the knowledge stands both goals stop at structure on the same two kinds of line of their own restatements, and with every condition item withheld the two built activities collapse into one plain GF2 activity (identical coach text; identical configuration lines but one transition value's source). So the existing relationships appear capable of the reuse Christian describes; what keeps the two contexts from collapsing is the missing operational interpretation and carrier of each context's condition, not a missing goal-family structure; each context's affordance targets stay deferred.
Implemented and tested: TA02 run through the same consumption (TA02-0, the knowledge as it stands; TA02-D, the diagnostic), over our DRAFT restatement of RPC-003 and the DRAFT TA02 record (TEST-ONLY draft store): its Game Problem, Game Form and constraints read CONSUMED. Measured from both goals' results (appendix C):

UNRESOLVED (10; kept explicit, never closed by an assumption; the same list stands on every run)
- UN-ORIGIN [UNRESOLVED] the origin clause of the approved condition (RPC-PROP-133): the sequence begins under established coordinated pressure: no carrier: the only typed begin condition is a restart (register V5: the begin condition of a restart, a STOP_RESUME transition), the only episode scope is BUILD_OUT_EPISODE, RPC-001's (SD-36), and V5 excludes a player's position during play; our restatement carries the clause as RPC-002-02.a with its referent as open text (RPC-002-02.b) and its value declared NOT_AUTHORED, and the knowledge run is refused on that line (c:restated:RPC-002:RPC-002-02.a::V6)
- UN-PRESSING-LINE [UNRESOLVED] the referent of the progression clause: the pressing line: open text in our restatement (RPC-002-03.b); RPC-002 authors no marked pressing line, the defenders' line in play is a player position (excluded from V5), and typing it as the target the scoring team attacks would replace the pressing line by the target, which the C54 distinction keeps apart; not typed by us. The value 'beyond' is carried (RPC-002-03.c)
- UN-CONTROL-REFERENT [UNRESOLVED] the referent of the control clause: retains possession through the next action: not stated: UNDECLARED in our restatement, so the knowledge run and A02-1 are refused on c:restated:RPC-002:RPC-002-04.a::V5; A02-1b and A02-2 pass that line only under the TEST-ONLY declaration NO_FURTHER_VALUE (TT-CONTROL-NO-FURTHER-VALUE: the C55 analog; not ruled; would need Christian's approval under M6). The value is carried (RPC-002-04.b)
- UN-SCOPE [UNRESOLVED] the scope of the condition: every condition item is scoped WHOLE_GAME (OURS), which holds every score of the game to the condition; the faithful scope is a High Press Escape sequence, which no vocabulary holds (an episode scope is added only by Christian's approval, as SD-36 added BUILD_OUT_EPISODE)
- UN-WORDING [UNRESOLVED] coach wording for the condition: none approved: A02-2 is refused at communication, awaiting approved wording for the control condition's type and value
- UN-RESTART-POSSESSION [UNRESOLVED] the restart and change-of-possession half of the starting state: SD-14 (Christian's decision of 18 September) makes a change of possession begin a new attacking episode, and defines episode boundaries only; Christian's caution of 8 November says a restart, a change of possession and a new attack are not automatically equivalent; GF2-17 makes play continue on a change of possession. Features of GF2 bearing on established pressure (OURS, no ruling): possession changes continue (GF2-17), and one shared target is attacked by both teams (GF2-12.a), so nothing makes one team the pressing team. Not closed here
- UN-LAWS [UNRESOLVED] the Laws for A02, offside (LAW-11): our DRAFT classification reads LAW-11 MATERIAL (its reason: players could wait there and bypass the interaction with opposition pressure), so A02-1b is refused at validation on it; A02-2 and A02-D pass only under the TEST-ONLY stand-in TT-LAW-11-STAND-IN; Christian's provisional no-offside ruling applies to the initial small-sided realization and is not generalized here; no ruling settles LAW-11 for A02

APPENDIX B: THE SAFEGUARDS IN DETAIL
REASONING-DISCONNECTION SAFEGUARDS (TEST-ONLY review evidence; OURS, for Christian's review)

DECISION OWNERSHIP
- the Game Problem: owner Step 1; input the goal id, the approved translation or a scoped ruling, the routing; output the resolved primary Game Problem, carried by the chain beside its own reading; authority the approved translation, or the scoped ruling it rests on
- the Game Problem reading that governs the engine lens hints and matched signals: owner Step 1 (an open dependency: the chain still reads the planning model itself); input the planning model, read inside the guided selection hints; output the lens hints and the matched signals the resolution check reads; authority none over an owned decision: reported, never switched
- the Representative Performance Context: owner Step 1; input the routing of the planning model; output the context whose approved rows govern scoring; authority the routing; the chain reads it itself (no consumption record exists for it)
- Game Form eligibility and choice: owner Step 2, under D3 over CANDIDATE cells; input Step 1, the context form rows, the form Game Problem cells, D3; output SELECTED, retained alternatives or none; the chain's candidate forms filtered to it (an intersection); authority Step 2 under D3; each cell keeps its own grade (a CANDIDATE cell is never printed as approved, preferred or sufficient)
- the constraints (manipulations): owner Christian's authorization record, or a TEST-ONLY record whose finding is ours (printed distinctly); input the record's authorized set (the record route); output the selection's candidate pool and the constraints built; authority Christian's ruling where Christian's record governs; none where the record is a TEST-ONLY one of ours
- whether the selection commits: owner the owned decisions above; an engine rule may refuse only as a check kept under the authorization's section 5; input the consumed form, the authorized set and the engine lenses; output a committed selection, or a refusal with the rule that refused; authority none found for the engine rules (reported with source and authority)
- the context's approved identity and scoring, clause by clause: owner the context's approved rows, through our CANDIDATE restatement; input the restated items that constitute each clause of the cited approved row; output the clause carried into the built game, withheld by a declared treatment, or not carried; authority the approved row; the restatement is ours and unreviewed
- what the account displays about the approved condition: owner the same approved row; input the account's context display; output whether the account says the condition is in this activity; authority the approved row; a display is never a pass
- realization choices and justified defaults: owner the chain, legitimately; input the consumed contracts and the realization choices; output the configuration lines, the text and the diagram; authority derives; closes open lines within authored bounds (graded line by line by the handoff)

APPENDIX D: THE CASES AS THE HANDOFF REPORTS THEM (the A02 runs and the TA02 runs: inputs, Steps 1 and 2, the form, the chain, configuration lines, coach text, laws and requirement accounts, refusals and what they rest on)
HANDOFF REPORT: the isolated reusable configuration handoff (TEST-ONLY review evidence)

What this is: the review pathway's Steps 1 and 2 handed to the checked configuration and rendering chain, case by case, with every line and sentence graded by what determined it. Engineering evidence only: passing these tests is not evidence of ecological suitability or of representative learning quality in the field. Nothing here is served to a coach, written to production knowledge, or an authorization.
Every configuration below: Productive, recurring opportunities to meet the intended Game Problem: not established (no approved relationship ties a Game Problem to any line).
How to read the coach text (OURS): 'wording APPROVED' means the sentence is, word for word, one of Christian's approved coach sentences; it says nothing about the standing of what it states (its class). 'wording DRAFT' means words that are not approved (the renderer's DRAFT wording, or the review-only wording of decision 1).
The renderer's own status words: DERIVED, required by the selected knowledge and worded as a requirement of the game; STARTING, where this activity starts (a value chosen inside authored bounds, an alternative taken, or a preferred default), said as a fact about this activity and adjustable; COACH_CHOICE, left to the coach and put to the coach as a choice.
Quotation marks: double, Christian's words, verbatim, with their received date; single, ours (labels, values, record text such as an item's evidence, and engine sentences, whose own double quotation marks are printed as single ones).
Diagram words (the labels of the diagram's dimensions, targets and teams) are compared and printed with their status, CHRISTIANS_TERM (the diagram's value for one of Christian's terms, printed under this token) or DRAFT, and the sentence of the coach text that carries them, where one does.

Christian's six classes:
   class 1: Approved canonical knowledge.
   class 2: An explicit scoped ruling.
   class 3: Candidate or provisional knowledge.
   class 4: A stated test assumption.
   class 5: An illustrative configuration choice.
   class 6: An unsupported or unresolved relationship.

The grading map (OURS, for Christian's review): each line takes the weakest class among its determining premises; each premise is printed with its own standing and, for a restatement, its source's standing beside it.
   RESTATED_ITEM: CANDIDATE; printed 'our restatement of {source}'; a contract item we restated (basis AUTHORED) from a source; the source's own standing is printed beside it, never this item's
   KNOWLEDGE_RULING_ITEM: RULING; printed 'RULING'; a knowledge item with basis OWNER_RULING (class 3 where the ruling is provisional)
   ASSUMED_ITEM: OURS; printed 'OURS: assumption in our restatement'; a contract item with basis ASSUMED; what rests on it inherits it
   ENGINE_ONLY_ITEM: OURS; printed 'OURS: engine convention'; a contract item with basis ENGINE_ONLY
   DRAFT_ITEM: CANDIDATE; printed 'DRAFT knowledge (ours, unreviewed)'; an item of a DRAFT contract read from the TEST-ONLY draft store
   REALIZATION_ITEM: RULING; printed 'RULING'; an item of a provisional realization quoting Christian, graded by its ruling (class 3 where provisional)
   STANDING_DECISION: RULING; printed 'RULING'; an engine standing decision that records a ruling of Christian's (the table is data, checked against the specification); one that records none is OURS: engine convention
   CASE_CHOICE_RULING: RULING; printed 'RULING'; a choice the case supplies for an open line, on a ruling of Christian's (class 3 where provisional)
   CASE_CHOICE_ILLUSTRATIVE: ILLUSTRATIVE; printed 'ILLUSTRATIVE (for this offline test only; to be replaced before any production use)'; a choice the case supplies for an open line, illustrative
   FILE_CHOICE_RULING: RULING; printed 'RULING'; a choice from a realization-choices file whose entries quote Christian (class 3 where provisional)
   FILE_CHOICE_OURS: OURS; printed 'OURS: a realization choice of ours, not knowledge'; a choice from a realization-choices file that says it is a realizer's input, not knowledge
   COACH: no standing; printed 'the coach decides'; a line the chain leaves to the coach (closure COACH_DECIDES): no class claimed
   ASSUMED_BOUND: OURS; printed 'OURS: assumption in our restatement'; the authored bound a justified default closes a line within, where that bound is ASSUMED
   STAND_IN: no standing; printed '{declared}'; a TEST-ONLY stand-in item, graded by the label its input declares (ILLUSTRATIVE class 5, TEST ASSUMPTION class 4), never by the item's own basis
   SESSION_ILLUSTRATIVE: ILLUSTRATIVE; printed 'ILLUSTRATIVE (for this offline test only; to be replaced before any production use)'; a session input entered offline
   SESSION_COACH_INPUT: RULING; printed 'coach input, within a ruled bound'; a coach's session input within ruled testing boundaries: the bound's ruling gives the class
   LAWS_DEFAULT_PRINCIPLE: RULING; printed 'RULING'; Christian's standing principle that the Laws apply by default
   LAWS_ENCODING: CANDIDATE; printed 'DRAFT: our per-law encoding'; what each law requires here, as our DRAFT encoding says
   LAW_DEPARTURE: RULING; printed 'RULING'; a departure from a law recorded on a provisional realization, by its ruling (class 3 where provisional)
   FORM_SELECTED: no standing; printed '{weakest of Step 2}'; the form Step 2 SELECTED, graded by the weakest standing among what decided it
   FORM_ILLUSTRATIVE: ILLUSTRATIVE; printed 'ILLUSTRATIVE (for this offline test only; to be replaced before any production use)'; a form the case names as illustrative, permitted by a filed ruling and checked among the compatible forms
   FORM_INFERENCE: OURS; printed 'OURS: LOGICAL INFERENCE (our reading, not canonical; needs Christian's confirmation)'; a form the case names on our logical inference, checked among the compatible forms
   UNRESOLVED: UNRESOLVED; printed 'UNRESOLVED: no authorized value; needs a knowledge decision'; a line no premise determines; the missing basis is named

Decision kinds A, B and C (OURS, for Christian's review; computed per line by this rule, with the case's contextual overrides):
   A where choosing differently, within what the form and the Laws permit, would materially change the affordance landscape, opposition, scoring consequences, challenge or representative information: here, a line chosen among alternatives, a default that has an alternative, a law departed from, or a value carried by a stand-in;
   B where the value is entailed by the selected form, the Sport Profile, an engine standing decision or the Laws' default, or is a necessary adaptation (still scrutinized, never exempt);
   C where several values are admissible and none changes the above: here, a line the chain leaves to the coach; any functional bound on it stays B;
   session players, dimensions and duration are contextual A by the case's overrides; a line nothing establishes is no configuration value and has no kind.
The examination of the existing structures (OURS, for Christian's review):
   What the existing structures already mark, per line: where a value may come from (the register's source kinds and fill permissions); its authority (Christian's six classes; a contract item's basis); how binding it is (strictness, value status, a derived line's strength); who decides it (the chain's coach-decided closure; a record's authorized adjustments); what the coach must be told (the overview essentials table); and, for the Laws only, whether an owed adaptation is material (our DRAFT classification).
   None of them alone separates the three kinds: a coach-adjustable line can be learning-governing, a structurally checked part can be learning-governing, a material law adaptation merges meaning with structural validity, and the illustrative class holds all three kinds.
   What suffices without a new library or layer: one field in the handoff's output (each line's kind, computed by the rule above from the markers the chain already writes) and per-case overrides in the review data where the boundary is contextual. Routine choices (bib colours, spare balls, marking equipment, the coach's position, delivery details) are not configuration values here: the handoff holds no value, no ruling and no line for them, and leaves them to the coach.
